  `id` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `matelev` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `matiere` varchar(15) NOT NULL,
  `anelev` varchar(12) NOT NULL,

  `dateabs` varchar(20) NOT NULL,

  `heurenreg` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `typeutil` int NOT NULL,
  `idleiter` int NOT NULL,
  `motensgt` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `nomenreg` varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb3;

--
-- Structure de la table `emploi`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `emploi`;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `emploi` (
  `ide` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `idh` int NOT NULL,
  `matier` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `class` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `an` varchar(15) NOT NULL,
  `idjour` int NOT NULL,
  `id_etab` int NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`ide`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=latin1;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Structure de la table `enseigmatclasse`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `enseigmatclasse`;

  `id` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `idmat` int NOT NULL,
  `idcl` int NOT NULL,
  `idens` int NOT NULL,
  `anens` varchar(11) NOT NULL,
  `titulaire` int NOT NULL,
  `sup` int NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb3;

--
-- Structure de la table `enseignant`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `enseignant`;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `enseignant` (
  `id` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `nomens` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `prenomens` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `telens` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `mtpasse` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `anens` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `genre` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `datens` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `cni` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `temps` int NOT NULL,
  `supp` int NOT NULL,
  `emailens` varchar(255) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb3;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Structure de la table `trimestre`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `trimestre`;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `trimestre` (
  `id` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `code` int NOT NULL,
  `nom` varchar(15) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb3;
COMMIT;
